def solve_local_lp(h, n, active_lags, max_constr=300):

    d=2/n.
    F = c_vec[n-1+lag]
    Build G rows for each lag.
    A_ub = hstack(G, -ones(M,1)); b=-F
    A_eq = zeros(1,n+1); A_eq[0,:n]=1
    bounds = [(-h_i,1-h_i)] + [(None,None)]
    res = linprog(c=zeros, A_ub=A_ub,b_ub=b,A_eq=A_eq,b_eq=0,bounds=bounds, method='highs')
    if success: step=res.x[:n]
    # For numerical, project step to bounds and zero sum? linprog already but due to tol. Clean.
    return step

def grad_shift(h,n,k):
 d=2/n; g=zeros(n)
 # first: indices i such that 0<=i-k<n => i in [max(0,k), min(n,k+n)) = if k>=0: k..n-1; if k<0: 0..n+k-1? Since k=-a: i in [0,n-a-1]? 0<=i+a<n => i<n-a. yes.
 lo1=max(0,k); hi1=min(n,k+n)
 g[lo1:hi1] += d*(1 - h[lo1 - k: hi1 - k])  # i-k
 # second: 0<=i+k<n => i in [max(0,-k), min(n,n-k))
 lo2=max(0,-k); hi2=min(n,n-k)
 g[lo2:hi2] -= d*h[(lo2+k):(hi2+k)]
 return g
This matches previous but with swapped? For k>=0: lo1=k,hi1=n: g[k:n]+=1-h[0:n-k]; lo2=0,hi2=n-k: g[0:n-k]-=h[k:n]. This is our corrected. For k<0 a=-k: lo1=0,hi1=n-a: g[0:n-a]+=1-h[a:n]; lo2=a,hi2=n: g[a:n]-=h[0:n-a]. Good.

Objective active smooth:
```
def active_smooth_obj(x,n,active_idx,alpha,mu):
 c = values_at_shifts(x,n,active_idx)
 m = c.max()
 sh = alpha*(c-m)
 smx=sh.max()
 if smx>500: logsum = smx+np.log(np.sum(np.exp(sh-smx)))
 else: logsum = np.log(np.sum(np.exp(sh)))
 val = m + logsum/alpha
 val += mu*(x.sum()-n/2)**2
 return float(val)
```
Smooth approximates max over active with small bias. At optimum of active smooth, active values likely equalized.

Let's design core functions in detail.

Feasibility:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    target=n/2.0
    h=np.clip(h.astype(float),0,1)
    for _ in range(1000):
       s=h.sum(); diff=target-s
       if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
       if diff>0:
          room=1-h; tot=room.sum()
          if tot<1e-15: break
          # add proportionally, but may clip; iterative
          h += (diff/tot)*room
          h=np.clip(h,0,1)
       else:
          tot=s
          if tot<1e-15: break
          h += (diff/tot)*h
          h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    return h
```
This can leave sum slightly off. Final `enforce_sum_exact`:
```
def enforce_sum_exact(h,n):
    target=n/2.0
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>0:
       # choose index with h between 0,1 far from bounds
       cand=np.where((h>1e-12)&(h<1-1e-12))[0]
       if cand.size:
          idx=cand[np.argmax(np.minimum(h[cand],1-h[cand]))]
          h[idx]+=diff
          if h[idx]<0 or h[idx]>1:
             # distribute? fallback iterative
             h[idx]=min(max(h[idx],0),1); return project_feasible(h,n)
    # after adjust, clip and verify
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>0:
       # add to largest room proportionally many times
       for _ in range(100):
          if diff>0:
             room=1-h; tot=room.sum(); if tot<1e-17: break
             inc=np.minimum(room, np.maximum(diff,0)/tot*room)?
          ...
    return h
```
Could use `np.nextafter`? The evaluator likely tolerance; not exact bitwise. But phrase "sum(h) == n_points/2 exactly" maybe they check close. We'll make sum very close. Since n and h floats, can't guarantee exact equality. But we can make one element exactly target - sum_others? Let's do:
```
def finalize_h(h,n):
    h=np.clip(h,0,1).copy()
    target=n/2.0
    # sort indices with 0<h<1; adjust one to make sum close
    # Use Kahan? Compute s = h.sum(); residual=target-s; choose idx with h in (residual directions)
    if residual !=0:
       # if residual positive, add to element with max room; else subtract from max value
       if residual>0:
          idx=int(np.argmax(1-h))
       else:
          idx=int(np.argmax(h))
       h[idx]+=residual
    # clip; if outside, use iterative scaling
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # correct residual again using fraction on multiple indices? The sum after clip may differ.
    for _ in range(100):
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
       if diff>0:
          room=1-h; tot=room.sum(); if tot<1e-18: break
          # add equally to all room? Could clip.
          h += (diff/tot)*room
          h=np.clip(h,0,1)
       else:
          tot=h.sum(); if tot<1e-18: break
          h += (diff/tot)*h
          h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # final single adjust without clipping if possible
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-16:
       if diff>0:
          idx=int(np.argmax(1-h)); room=1-h[idx]
       else:
          idx=int(np.argmax(h)); room=h[idx]
       step = min(room, abs(diff))
       if diff>0: h[idx]+=step
       else: h[idx]-=step
       # if still off, distribute
       diff=target-h.sum()
       # if small, do Kahan? not important.
    return h
```
The evaluator computes sum(h)*dx; if off by 1e-14 okay. We can also set one element using `h[idx] = h[idx] + (target - float(np.sum(np.delete(h, idx), dtype=np.float64)))` but delete O(n). Not needed.

Evaluation:
```
def compute_c5_vec(h,n): return np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full') * (2.0/n)
def compute_c5(h,n): return float(np.max(compute_c5_vec(h,n)))
```
Potential performance: `1.0-h` allocation each eval. Fine.

Values at active shifts:
```
def c_values_active(h,n,active_idx):
    dx=2.0/n; out=np.empty(len(active_idx))
    for r,idx in enumerate(active_idx):
       k=idx-(n-1)
       if k>=0:
          m=n-k
          out[r]=dx*np.dot(h[k:k+m], 1.0 - h[:m])
       else:
          a=-k; m=n-a
          out[r]=dx*np.dot(h[:m], 1.0 - h[a:a+m])
    return out
```
Check for k negative: F_k=Σ_{q=-k}^{n-1} h_{q+k}(1-h_q). q from a to n-1, q+k = q-a. h[:m], h[a:a+m], yes.

`shift_gradient` as above. Use dx. To speed, maybe returns row.
```
def shift_gradient(h,n,k):
    d=2.0/n; g=np.zeros(n)
    lo1=max(0,k); hi1=min(n,k+n)
    if lo1<hi1: g[lo1:hi1] += d*(1.0 - h[lo1-k:hi1-k])
    lo2=max(0,-k); hi2=min(n,n-k)
    if lo2<hi2: g[lo2:hi2] -= d*h[lo2+k:hi2+k]
    return g
```
Check lo1-k: if k=-a, lo1=0, hi1=n-a, h[a:n]. Good. lo1-k length hi1-lo1. For k positive, h[0:n-k]. Good. lo2: for k pos n-k, h[k:n]. For k neg a, lo2=a, hi2=n, h[0:n-a]. Good.

`build_jacobian` can use `G=np.empty((2*n-1,n))`; for idx. But if we only need active, use `grad_rows_active`.

Active selection:
```
def select_active(c_vec,n,count,margin=0.0):
    order=np.argsort(c_vec)[::-1]
    # include top count and maybe values within margin of max
    maxv=c_vec.max()
    if margin>0:
       inside=np.where(c_vec >= maxv - margin)[0]
       order=np.unique(np.concatenate((inside, order[:count])))
    else:
       order=order[:count]
    return np.array(sorted(order),dtype=int)
```
Actually sorted not needed. For LP constraints order doesn't matter. For L-BFGS active, sorted maybe stable.

LP step:
```
from scipy.optimize import linprog

def local_lp_step(h,n,active_idx, max_constr=300, margin=1e-5):
    c = compute_c5_vec(h,n) # full maybe for current
    # choose active maybe top of c
    active_idx = select_active(c,n,max_constr,margin? )
    if len(active_idx)==0: return None
    Gact = grad_rows_active(h,n,active_idx)
    F = c_values_active(h,n,active_idx) # should equal c[active_idx]
    A_ub = np.hstack((Gact, -np.ones((len(active_idx),1))))
    b_ub = -F
    A_eq = np.zeros((1,n+1)); A_eq[0,:n]=1
    bounds = [(-h[i], 1-h[i]) for i in range(n)] + [(None,None)]
    c_obj = np.zeros(n+1); c_obj[-1]=1
    # To avoid unbounded z if LPIter? bounds on z maybe [-2,2]
    bounds[-1] = (-2.0, 2.0)
    res=linprog(c_obj,A_ub=A_ub,b_ub=b_ub,A_eq=A_eq,b_eq=[0],bounds=bounds,method='highs')
    if not res.success or 'iteration' in res.message? Check.
    delta=res.x[:n]
    # If delta small return None
    if np.max(np.abs(delta))<1e-12: return None
    # optionally clip to trust radius? 
    return delta
```
Bounds list of n+1 tuples creates overhead; for n=1000 okay. Could use array `bounds = np.column_stack((-h,1-h)); bounds=np.vstack( (bounds, [ [-2,2] ] ) )` but need different None? Can set z bounds (-1,1). Use list comprehension perhaps okay but no closures. For n=1000, 1001 tuples per LP; okay. But many LPs; maybe use array faster:
```
lb = np.empty(n+1); ub=np.empty(n+1)

Line search for delta:
```
def line_search_step(h,n,best_c,delta,max_try=10):
    alphas=[1,0.5,0.25,...]
    for a in alphas:
       h_new = h + a*delta
       h_new = project_feasible(h_new,n) # may slightly change sum/direction
       c_new=compute_c5(h_new,n)
       if c_new < best_c - 1e-12:
           return h_new,c_new,a
       if a<0.01: break
    return h,best_c,0
```

Multi-start perturbations:
At high resolution, generate `num_restarts` based on time (e.g., 4). For each, perturb current best, run short optimize (LP + L-BFGS) with ~30-60s, keep best. Perturb methods:
- `perturb_smooth(h,n,rng, amp)`: add random sine/cosine series with random coefficients decaying. Maintain sum.
- `perturb_blocks`: split into B blocks (10-50), add random uniform noise per block, project.
- `perturb_random_irreproducible`: choose random sparse coordinate transfers: for m pairs, transfer small mass, project. This can emulate local global moves.
Use `np.random.default_rng(seed+r)`.

Random perturbations may break symmetry but find better basin. Since parent good, small amplitude (0.02-0.1). Project. Evaluate; if C not too worse (e.g., < best+0.01), optimize. If much worse, skip.

But with time, too many restarts may not improve if local optimizer strong. Maybe 3 restarts.

# helpers...

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    deadline=t0+budget_s-5
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    # init
    try:
       h_parent=np.asarray(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy()
       n_parent=len(h_parent)
    except Exception:
       n_parent=500
       h_parent=np.full(500,0.5)
    h_parent=project_feasible(h_parent,n_parent)
    best_h=h_parent.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h_parent,n_parent)

    # If parent small, optimize at parent original maybe 60s? But parent already optimized? Use for early.
    if n_parent <= 1000:
       t1=min(t0+120, deadline-400)
       h0,c0=optimize_resolution(h_parent,n_parent,t1, max_phase='medium')
       if c0<best_c: update

    # choose work n

       n_work = n_parent
       h_work = h_parent
    else:
       n_work = 1000
       h_work = upsample(best_h, best_n?, n_work)

       # always use h_work for optimization even if worse? maybe yes

    # refine to n_refine if time and best not target

       h_ref,c_ref=optimize_resolution(h_ref,n_refine,deadline-40, max_phase='light')
       if c_ref<best_c: update
    # final exact enforce

    return best_h,best_c,len(best_h)

def upsample_h(h,n):
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,len(h))
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n)
    return np.interp(x_new,x_old,h)

LP run function:
```
def run_lp(h,n,deadline,lp_k=150,margin=0.0):
   h=project_feasible(h,n); best_c=compute_c5(h,n); best_h=h.copy()
   for it in range(200):
       rem=deadline-time.time()
       if rem<5: break
       c_full=compute_c5_vec(h,n); C=c_full.max()
       if C<best_c: best_c=C; best_h=h.copy()
       active=select_active(c_full,n,lp_k,margin)
       # if active count 0 break
       delta=solve_lp_step(h,n,active)
       if delta is None: break
       # line search
       h_new,c_new,accepted=line_search(h,n,best_c,delta)
       if accepted:
           h=h_new; best_c=c_new; best_h=h.copy()
       else:
           # try half? line search includes; break or try another active set (expand/shrink)
           # maybe use active top only one? 
           break
   return best_h,best_c
```

Active L-BFGS run:
```
def run_active_lbfgs(h,n,deadline,active_count=80,alpha=2000,mu=10.0,outer_calls=3):
    h=project_feasible(h,n); best_c=compute_c5(h,n); best_h=h.copy()
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0) for _ in range(n)]
    for outer in range(outer_calls):
       rem=deadline-time.time()
       if rem<20: break
       c=compute_c5_vec(h,n); active=select_active(c,n,active_count,0.0)
       # maybe add negative? okay
       time_slice=min(rem, 45)
       dea=time.time()+time_slice
       try:
          res=minimize(active_smooth_obj,h,jac=active_smooth_grad,args=(n,active,alpha,mu),method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=bounds,options={'maxiter':500,'ftol':1e-18,'gtol':1e-12,'maxfun':500,'maxls':30})
          h_new=project_feasible(res.x,n); c_new=compute_c5(h_new,n)
          if c_new<best_c: update
       except: pass
    return best_h,best_c
```

But each operation creates arrays? We can optimize feasible(T) by precomputing pos/neg masks for a? Since a changes per pair. We can do:
```
def feasible_T(c,a,T,low,high,eps=1e-13):
   L=low; U=high
   pos = a > eps
   if np.any(pos):
      u = np.min((T-c[pos])/a[pos])
      if u < U: U = u
   neg = a < -eps
   if np.any(neg):
      l = np.max((T-c[neg])/a[neg])
      if l > L: L = l
   zero_mask = (a <= eps) & (a >= -eps)
   if np.any(zero_mask) and c[zero_mask].max() > T: return None
   if L <= U: return L,U
   return None
```

`select_active(c, n, count, margin, cap)`:
```
def select_active(c,n,count,margin=0.0,cap=None):
    idx=np.argsort(c)[::-1]
    if cap is None: cap=max(count, min(len(c), 500))
    if margin>0:
       maxv=c.max()
       inside=np.where(c >= maxv - margin)[0]
       idx=np.unique(np.concatenate((inside, idx[:count])))
       if len(idx)>cap:
           # keep top by c
           idx=idx[np.argsort(-c[idx])][:cap]
    else:
       idx=idx[:min(count,len(c))]
    return idx.astype(int)
```
Could margin 0.005 include many; cap maybe 300.

`active_softmax_weights(c, alpha)`:
```
def softmax_weights(c,alpha):
    m=c.max()
    sh=alpha*(c-m)
    mx=sh.max()
    if mx>500:
       w=np.exp(sh-mx)
       w/=w.sum()
       logsum=mx+np.log(w.sum()) # wait if normalized? Better:
    else: ...
```
But for objective we need logsum and weights. Implement helper `logsumexp_alpha(c,alpha)` returns logsum, weights.
```
def logsumexp_weights(c,alpha):
    m=c.max(); sh=alpha*(c-m)
    mx=sh.max()
    expsh=np.exp(sh-mx)
    s=expsh.sum()
    w=expsh/s
    logsum=mx+np.log(s)
    return m+logsum/alpha, w
```
Because logΣ exp(α(c-m)) = mx + log(s). Value = m + logsum/α. Good.

`run_lp`:
```
def run_lp(h,n,deadline,lp_k=150,margin=0.0,trust=0.1,max_iter=200):
   h=project_feasible(h,n); best_c=compute_c5(h,n); best_h=h.copy()
   for it in range(max_iter):
      rem=deadline-time.time()
      if rem<8: break
      c=compute_c5_vec(h,n)
      C=float(c.max())
      if C<best_c:
         best_c=C; best_h=h.copy()
      active=select_active(c,n,lp_k,margin,cap=min(len(c),600))
      if len(active)<2: break
      delta=local_lp_step(h,n,active,trust)
      if delta is None:
         # maybe try smaller active top only
         delta=local_lp_step(h,n,active[:10],trust)
         if delta is None: break
      h2,c2,ok=line_search_delta(h,n,best_c,delta)
      if ok:
         h=h2; best_c=c2; best_h=h.copy()
         # if improvement small many? continue
      else:
         break
   return best_h,best_c
```
If line search fails, another active set might work; we can before break try active with margin=0 (top 50). Then break.

`run_slqp_polish`:
```
def slqp_obj(x,n,active_idx,alpha): return active_smooth_obj(x,n,active_idx,alpha,0.0)
def slqp_grad(x,n,active_idx,alpha): return active_smooth_grad(x,n,active_idx,alpha,0.0)
def sum_eq(x,n): return float(x.sum()-n/2.0)
def sum_jac(x,n): return np.ones(len(x))
def run_slqp_polish(h,n,deadline):
   h=project_feasible(h,n); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
   c=compute_c5_vec(h,n); active=select_active(c,n,60,0.0,cap=60)
   bounds=[(0,1)]*n
   cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':sum_eq,'jac':sum_jac,'args':(n,)}]
   try:
      res=minimize(slqp_obj,h,jac=slqp_grad,args=(n,active,5000.0),method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,options={'maxiter':200,'ftol':1e-12})
      h_new=project_feasible(res.x,n)
      c_new=compute_c5(h_new,n)
      if c_new<best_c: return h_new,c_new
   except: pass
   return h,best_c
```

Pair polish implementation:
```
def feasible_interval(c,a,T,low,high,eps=1e-12):
   L=low; U=high
   pos = a > eps
   if np.any(pos):
       bound = np.min((T - c[pos]) / a[pos])
       if bound < U: U = bound
   neg = a < -eps
   if np.any(neg):
       bound = np.max((T - c[neg]) / a[neg])
       if bound > L: L = bound
   if L > U: return None
   # zero: a within eps; use np.where not pos/neg
   zero = ~(pos | neg)
   if np.any(zero) and c[zero].max() > T + 1e-14:
       return None
   return L,U
```
This creates masks each iteration, expensive. Could precompute masks per pair but masks are based on a which is vector. In binary loop, pos/neg fixed; we can compute once outside! Good. `pair_line_search` can compute pos/neg/zero masks once:
```
pos = a > eps; neg = a < -eps; zero=~(pos|neg); zero_cmax = c[zero].max() if zero.any else -inf
for T:
  ... use c[pos],a[pos] arrays (precomputed) and c[neg],a[neg]
```
Precompute `c_pos,a_pos,c_neg,a_neg`. For zero, only c_zero_max. Then feasible fast.
```
def pair_line_search(c,a,low,high):
   if low>=high: return 0.0
   pos=a>1e-12; neg=a<-1e-12; zero=~(pos|neg)
   c_pos=c[pos]; a_pos=a[pos]; c_neg=c[neg]; a_neg=a[neg]
   c_zero_max = float(c[zero].max()) if zero.any() else -1.0
   highT=float(c.max()); lowT=0.0
   # if highT infeasible due to zero? at current δ=0 objective=max c, should feasible; but if low/high excludes 0? low<=0<=high? For pair bounds, δ=0 always feasible. At T=c.max, for pos: (T-c)/a >=0? Since T>=c, yes bound >=0. For neg: (T-c)/a <=0? If T>=c, numerator >=0, denominator neg => <=0; L max with low maybe 0 or negative. Interval includes 0. Good.
   last_interval=None
   for _ in range(45):
      mid=(lowT+highT)*0.5
      L=low; U=high
      if c_zero_max > mid + 1e-14: # infeasible
         lowT=mid; continue
      if a_pos.size:
         b=np.min((mid - c_pos)/a_pos)
         if b < U: U=b
      if a_neg.size:
         b=np.max((mid - c_neg)/a_neg)
         if b > L: L=b
      if L <= U:
         highT=mid; last_interval=(L,U)
      else:
         lowT=mid
   if last_interval is None: return 0.0
   L,U=last_interval
   if L <= 0 <= U: return 0.0
   if L > 0: return L
   return U
```
Need if highT initial maybe C but feasible. Good.
Optionally if minT close to C, delta maybe 0 if interval includes 0; if minT lower and interval doesn't. Good.

`pair_polish`:
```
def build_jacobian(h,n):
    S=2*n-1; G=np.empty((S,n),dtype=float)
    for idx in range(S): G[idx]=gradient_shift(h,n,idx-(n-1))
    return G

def run_pair_polish(h,n,deadline,pair_count=20,rebuild_every=10,max_sweeps=5):
   h=project_feasible(h,n); best_c=compute_c5(h,n); best_h=h.copy()
   c=compute_c5_vec(h,n)
   G=build_jacobian(h,n)
   for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
      rem=deadline-time.time()
      if rem<60: break
      active=select_active(c,n,50,0.002,cap=100)
      if len(active)<2: break
      # weights softmax alpha=5000
      fav=c[active]; m=fav.max(); w=np.exp(5000*(fav-m)); w/=w.sum()
      g=G[active].T @ w
      # candidates high and low
      order=np.argsort(g)
      low_idx=order[:pair_count]
      high_idx=order[::-1][:pair_count]
      accepted=0
      # also random 10 pairs?
      for i in high_idx:
         for j in low_idx:
            if i==j: continue
            low = max(-h[i], h[j]-1.0)
            high = min(1.0-h[i], h[j])
            if high - low < 1e-12: continue
            a = G[:,i] - G[:,j]
            delta=pair_line_search(c,a,low,high)
            if abs(delta)<1e-10: continue
            # apply
            h[i]+=delta; h[j]-=delta
            # clip if numerical
            if h[i]<0: h[i]=0; h[j] -=? But delta feasible; due to float.
            h[i]=min(max(h[i],0),1); h[j]=min(max(h[j],0),1)
            c2=compute_c5(h,n)
            if c2 < best_c - 1e-13:
               best_c=c2; best_h=h.copy(); c=compute_c5_vec(h,n); accepted+=1
               if accepted % rebuild_every == 0:
                  G=build_jacobian(h,n)
            else:
               # revert
               h[i]-=delta; h[j]+=delta
               h[i]=min(max(h[i],0),1); h[j]=min(max(h[j],0),1)
      # after sweep, if no accepted, break; else rebuild if not already? 
      if accepted==0: break
      G=build_jacobian(h,n); c=compute_c5_vec(h,n)
   return best_h,best_c
```
But if we update `c` within accepted and not rebuild G, subsequent pair a uses stale G but c updated. Coefficients stale. Could be okay. But if we update c after each acceptance, high cost but pair count small. We can rebuild G less often but c updated. Since a stale may lead to poor delta but line check rejects. Could we update a incrementally? Not necessary.

Simpler: random Gaussian smoothed by averaging.

def add_smooth_perturbation(h,n,rng,amp, length=10):
   noise=rng.normal(size=(n,))
   for _ in range(3): noise=np.convolve(noise, np.ones(length)/length, mode='same')
   h2=h+amp*noise
   return project_feasible(h2,n)
```

def add_block_perturbation(h,n,rng,amp,num_blocks=20):

   if len(b)<n: b=np.pad? simpler use np.repeat then trim.

Potential bug: `active_smooth_grad` weights and G. G rows are derivatives of c values. Softmax weights sum 1. Derivative of m+logsum/α exactly w? Let's prove: V = m + (1/α) log Σ_j exp(α(c_j-m)). Let M index max; e^{α(c_M-m)}=1. derivative V wrt c_l = (1/α)[ α w_l - α m'? m is max c, piecewise; but in logsumexp formulation V = (1/α) log Σ exp(α c_j) exactly if we use raw logsumexp, no explicit m. Our V = m + logsum(α(c-m))/α. Since m = max c, for a differentiable point where only one max and not tie, derivative of m is 1 for max, and logsum derivative α w; α(c-m) derivative α for max, -α for others? Let's compute: V = logsumexp(α c)/α = m + logΣ exp(α(c-m))/α. The standard gradient of logsumexp(αc)/α is w. Because derivative of logsum(α(c-m)) is Σ exp(...)(α - α if j is max? but m changes) = α. So yes gradient = w. Our implementation w from softmax weights; good.

But in `logsumexp_weights`, if we compute `w=exp(sh-mx)/s`, and return `m+logsum/alpha` where logsum=mx+log(s). This is correct. For alpha large, weights on max.

`select_active` cap: If margin includes many, we cap by top c values. Need `idx` sorted maybe. Implementation:
```
def select_active(c,n,count,margin=0.0,cap=300):
    order=np.argsort(c)[::-1]
    if margin>0:
       maxv=c.max()
       inside=np.where(c >= maxv - margin)[0]
       combined=np.unique(np.concatenate((inside, order[:count])))
       if len(combined)>cap:
           combined=combined[np.argsort(-c[combined])][:cap]
       return combined.astype(int)
    else:
       return order[:min(count,len(c))].astype(int)
```

Pseudo:
```
def optimize_resolution(h,n,deadline,mode='full'):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(h, n)
    # LP settings depending mode
    if mode == 'full':
       lp_settings = [(80,0.0,0.1,70), (160,0.003,0.1,80), (300,0.01,0.2,80)]
    elif mode == 'medium':
       lp_settings = [(80,0.0,0.1,45), (160,0.005,0.2,45)]
    else: # light
       lp_settings = [(80,0.0,0.1,25)]
    for count,margin,trust,slice_s in lp_settings:
       rem=deadline-time.time()
       if rem < slice_s+10: break
       dea=min(deadline, time.time()+slice_s)
       h2,c2=run_lp(h,n,dea,lp_k=count,margin=margin,trust=trust,max_iter=200)
       if c2<best_c:
          best_c=c2; best_h=h2
    # active lbfgs settings
    if mode == 'full':
       lbfgs_settings = [(40,500,35,4), (80,2000,45,4), (160,8000,45,3), (300,20000,60,2)]
    elif mode == 'medium':
       lbfgs_settings = [(60,1000,30,3), (160,5000,40,2)]
    else:
       lbfgs_settings = [(80,2000,25,2)]
    for count,alpha,slice_s,max_outer in lbfgs_settings:
       rem=...
       if rem< slice_s+10: break
       dea=min(deadline, time.time()+slice_s)
       h2,c2=run_active_lbfgs(h,n,dea,active_count=count,alpha=alpha,mu=10.0,max_outer=max_outer,slice_s=min(slice_s, max(10, rem//max_outer)))
       if c2<best_c: update
    # maybe second LP with new h
    if mode=='full':
       rem=...
       if rem>60: h2,c2=run_lp(h,n,deadline-50,lp_k=200,margin=0.005,trust=0.1)
       update
    # pair polish if full and rem>120 and n<=1500? Pair at n=2000 maybe okay but skip if heavy.
    if mode=='full' and time.time()<deadline-120:
       h2,c2=run_pair_polish(h,n,deadline-60,pair_count=15,rebuild_every=8,max_sweeps=3)
       update
    # SLSQP if full and rem>80 and n<=1200
    if mode=='full' and n<=1200 and time.time()<deadline-80:
       h2,c2=run_slqp_polish(h,n,deadline-10)
       update
    # final enforce
    best_h=enforce_sum_exact(best_h,n)
    best_c=compute_c5(best_h,n)
    return best_h,best_c
```

```
def enforce_sum_exact(h,n):
    h=np.clip(h.astype(float).copy(),0.0,1.0)
    target=n/2.0
    # first iterative projection
    for _ in range(1000):
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
       if diff>0:
          room=1.0-h
          tot=room.sum()
          if tot<1e-16: break
          h += (diff/tot)*room
          h=np.clip(h,0,1)
       else:
          tot=h.sum()
          if tot<1e-16: break
          h += (diff/tot)*h
          h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # final direct adjustment
    diff=target-float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff)>0:
       if diff>0:
          idx=int(np.argmax(1.0-h))
          room=float(1.0-h[idx])
          if room>=abs(diff): h[idx]+=diff
          else:
             h[idx]=1.0; diff-=room; # distribute residual? loop
             for _ in range(100):
                idx=int(np.argmax(1.0-h)); room=1-h[idx]
                step=min(room,-diff? actually diff positive)
                if step<=0: break
                h[idx]+=step; diff-=step
       else:
          idx=int(np.argmax(h))
          room=float(h[idx])
          if room>=abs(diff): h[idx]+=diff  # diff negative subtract
          else:
             h[idx]=0.0; diff+=room; #? 
             for ... subtract
    return h
```
This is messy. Simpler: Use project_feasible final with many iterations. Sim offline probably tolerance. We can just `return project_feasible(h,n)`. ```
def enforce_sum_exact(h,n):
   h=project_feasible(h,n)
   target=n/2.0
   # Use Bahadur: choose largest room element and adjust residual, then if sum off due to float, use multiple.
   for _ in range(10):
       s=float(np.sum(h, dtype=np.float64))
       diff=target-s
       if abs(diff)<=1e-15: break
       if diff>0:
          idx=int(np.argmax(1.0-h))
          add=min(diff, 1.0-h[idx]-1e-16)
       else:
          idx=int(np.argmax(h))
          add=max(diff, h[idx]+1e-16) # negative
       h[idx]+=add
       h=np.clip(h,0,1)
   return h
```

```
def is_valid_h(h,n):
   if h.size!=n: return False
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)): return False
   if np.min(h)<-1e-12 or np.max(h)>1+1e-12: return False
   if abs(h.sum()-n/2)>1e-6: return False
   return True
```

Top-level `smooth_noise(noise, iterations)`.
```
def smooth_noise(noise, iterations=3, radius=5):
    for _ in range(iterations):
       kernel = np.ones(2*radius+1)/(2*radius+1)
       noise = np.convolve(noise, kernel, mode='same')
    # normalize to zero mean
    noise -= noise.mean()
    norm = np.sqrt(np.mean(noise*noise))
    if norm>0: noise/=norm
    return noise
```

`project_feasible`:
```
def project_feasible(h, n):
    target = n / 2.0
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy(), 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(2000):
        diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = float(np.sum(room))
            if tot < 1e-16:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * room
            h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            tot = float(np.sum(h))
            if tot < 1e-16:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * h
            h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h
```
Potential if diff positive and room sum small but target infeasible? target half feasible, so total room = n - sum = target? if sum<target, room>target? okay.

`enforce_sum_exact`:
```
def enforce_sum_exact(h,n):
    h = project_feasible(h,n)
    target=n/2.0
    for _ in range(20):
        s=float(np.sum(h, dtype=np.float64))
        diff=target-s
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-14: break
        if diff > 0:
            idx=int(np.argmax(1.0-h))
            add=min(diff, max(0.0, 1.0-h[idx]-1e-18))
        else:
            idx=int(np.argmax(h))
            add=max(diff, min(0.0, h[idx]+1e-18)) # if h=0? but diff neg, room h
            # simpler: add=diff; if add < -h[idx]: add=-h[idx]
            add = diff
            if add < -h[idx] + 1e-18: add = -h[idx]
        h[idx] += add
        h = np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    return h
```
For diff positive, if all room too small? not. Use `min(diff, 1-h[idx]-1e-18)`. If add 0 due to 1e-18, break? okay. For diff negative: `add = diff; if add < -h[idx] + 1e-18: add = -h[idx]` where -h negative. If h=0 and diff neg, add 0; loop maybe but feasible. Better:
```
if diff<0:
  idx=argmax(h); room=h[idx]; add=diff; if add < -room: add=-room
```
Then if add==0 break.

`is_valid_h`:
```
def is_valid_h(h,n):
    if h.size!=n: return False
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)): return False
    if float(np.min(h)) < -1e-10 or float(np.max(h)) > 1.0+1e-10: return False
    if abs(float(np.sum(h))-n/2.0) > 1e-6: return False
    return True
```

`compute_c5_vec`:
```
def compute_c5_vec(h,n):
    dx=2.0/float(n)
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full') * dx
```
Could `h` not contiguous; okay.

`select_active` as above. Need if `count` > len. Ensure cap not too small. If margin combined empty? order. Good.

`values_at_shifts`:
```
def values_at_shifts(h,n,active_idx):
    dx=2.0/float(n)
    out=np.empty(len(active_idx), dtype=float)
    for r, idx in enumerate(active_idx):
       k=int(idx-(n-1))
       if k>=0:
          m=n-k
          a1=h[k:k+m]; a2=h[:m]
          out[r]=dx*(float(np.sum(a1)) - float(np.dot(a1,a2)))
       else:
          a=-k; m=n-a
          a1=h[:m]; a2=h[a:a+m]
          out[r]=dx*(float(np.sum(a1)) - float(np.dot(a1,a2)))
    return out
```
Check c for k=0: m=n, sum(h)-dot(h,h)=Σh(1-h) yes.

`shift_gradient_into`:
```
def shift_gradient_into(g,h,n,k):
    d=2.0/float(n)
    g[:]=0.0
    lo1 = max(0, k); hi1 = min(n, k+n)
    if lo1 < hi1:
        g[lo1:hi1] += d * (1.0 - h[lo1-k:hi1-k])
    lo2 = max(0, -k); hi2 = min(n, n-k)
    if lo2 < hi2:
        g[lo2:hi2] -= d * h[lo2+k:hi2+k]
```
Need check for negative k: hi1 = min(n,k+n) = n+a? if k=-a, k+n = n-a, good. lo1=0. lo1-k = a; hi1-k = n. For positive k: lo1=k,hi1=n, hi1-k=n-k. Good.
lo2: positive k: max0=0, min(n,n-k). lo2+k=k, hi2+k=n. Negative k: lo2=a, hi2=n, lo2+k=0, hi2+k=n-a. Good.

`grad_rows_active`:
```
def grad_rows_active(h,n,active_idx):
    P=len(active_idx)
    G=np.zeros((P,n), dtype=float)
    for r,idx in enumerate(active_idx):
        shift_gradient_into(G[r], h, n, int(idx-(n-1)))
    return G
```

`logsumexp_weights`:
```
def logsumexp_weights(c,alpha):
    m=float(np.max(c))
    sh=alpha*(c-m)
    mx=float(np.max(sh))
    expsh=np.exp(sh-mx)
    s=float(np.sum(expsh))
    if s <= 0.0:
       w=np.zeros_like(c); w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0
       return m, w
    w=expsh/s
    val = m + (mx + np.log(s)) / alpha
    return float(val), w
```
If c empty? Not. `active_smooth_obj` can handle P=0? maybe return 0.

`active_smooth_obj`:
```
def active_smooth_obj(x,n,active_idx,alpha,mu):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float)
    if len(active_idx)==0:
       val=0.0
    else:
       c=values_at_shifts(x,n,active_idx)
       val,_=logsumexp_weights(c, max(float(alpha),1.0))
    if mu != 0.0:
       val += float(mu)*float((x.sum()-n/2.0)**2)
    return float(val)
```
`mu` could be None? no.

`active_smooth_grad`:
```
def active_smooth_grad(x,n,active_idx,alpha,mu):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float)
    nint=int(n)
    if len(active_idx)==0:
       grad=np.zeros(nint)
    else:
       c=values_at_shifts(x,nint,active_idx)
       _,w=logsumexp_weights(c, max(float(alpha),1.0))
       G=grad_rows_active(x,nint,active_idx)
       grad=G.T @ w
    if mu != 0.0:
       grad = grad + 2.0*float(mu)*float(x.sum()-n/2.0)*np.ones(nint)
    return grad
```
Potential if x length n; okay.

`local_lp_step`:
```
def local_lp_step(h,n,active_idx,trust=0.1):
    if len(active_idx) < 2: return None
    G=grad_rows_active(h,n,active_idx)
    F=values_at_shifts(h,n,active_idx)
    M=len(active_idx)
    A_ub=np.empty((M,n+1), dtype=float)
    A_ub[:,:n]=G
    A_ub[:,n]=-1.0
    b_ub=-F
    c_obj=np.zeros(n+1); c_obj[n]=1.0
    A_eq=np.zeros((1,n+1)); A_eq[0,:n]=1.0
    lb=np.maximum(-h, -float(trust))
    ub=np.minimum(1.0-h, float(trust))
    lb_full=np.empty(n+1); ub_full=np.empty(n+1)
    lb_full[:n]=lb; ub_full[:n]=ub; lb_full[n]=-2.0; ub_full[n]=2.0
    bounds=list(zip(lb_full, ub_full))
    try:
       res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=np.zeros(1), bounds=bounds, method='highs')
    except Exception:
       return None
    if res.status not in (0,2) and not getattr(res,'success',False):
       return None
    delta=np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=float)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(delta)): return None
    delta=np.clip(delta, -h, 1.0-h)
    delta -= float(np.mean(delta))
    if float(np.max(np.abs(delta))) < 1e-12:
       return None
    return delta
```
`res.status` 0 optimal, 2 iteration limit? If iteration limit, maybe usable? success True for optimal; status 2 maybe? We'll accept if success. `res.status` maybe absent? scipy LinprogResult has success. Use `if not res.success: return None`. Status 0. If status 2 (iteration limit) success False? Actually status 2 iteration limit, success False but x maybe. Use `if not res.success and res.status not in (0,): return None`. Some versions: success True for status 0. Use `if not res.success: return None`. Simplicity. But if maxiter reached with finite x, could be useful. We can accept if `res.x` finite. `res.status` values: 0 optimal, 1 infeasible, 2 iteration limit, 3 unbounded, 4 numerical? If iteration limit, maybe x feasible. Could use. Let's use:
```
if res.status not in (0,2): return None
```
If no status? use success. `hasattr(res,'status')`.
```
ok = getattr(res,'status',1) in (0,2) or getattr(res,'success',False)
```
Good.

`line_search_delta`:
```
def line_search_delta(h,n,best_c,delta):
    alphas=(1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625,0.03125)
    for a in alphas:
       h2=h+a*delta
       # quick feasibility after projection
       h2=project_feasible(h2,n)
       if not is_valid_h(h2,n): continue
       c2=compute_c5(h2,n)
       if c2 < best_c - 1e-13:
          return h2,c2,True
    return h.copy(),best_c,False
```
If a*delta sum zero, project minimal. Good.

`run_lp`:
```
def run_lp(h,n,deadline,lp_k=120,margin=0.0,trust=0.1,max_iter=100):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
    if n > 1200:
       lp_k_eff=min(lp_k,150)
    else:
       lp_k_eff=min(lp_k,300)
    for it in range(max_iter):
       rem=deadline - time.time()
       if rem < 8.0: break
       c=compute_c5_vec(h,n)
       C=float(c.max())
       if C < best_c:
           best_c=C; best_h=h.copy()
       active=select_active(c,n,lp_k_eff,margin,cap=min(2*n-1, 600))
       if len(active) < 2: break
       delta=local_lp_step(h,n,active,trust)
       if delta is None:
           if lp_k_eff > 20:
               delta=local_lp_step(h,n,active[:max(2,min(20,len(active)))], trust*1.5)
           if delta is None:
               break
       h2,c2,ok=line_search_delta(h,n,best_c,delta)
       if ok:
           h=h2; best_c=c2; best_h=h.copy()
       else:
           # try double trust once
           if trust < 0.4 and len(active) >= 2:
               delta2=local_lp_step(h,n,active, min(0.6, trust*2.0))
               if delta2 is not None:
                  h2,c2,ok2=line_search_delta(h,n,best_c,delta2)
                  if ok2:
                     h=h2; best_c=c2; best_h=h.copy(); continue
           break
    return best_h,best_c
```
`continue` inside else okay. If fail double, break.

`run_active_lbfgs`:
```
def run_active_lbfgs(h,n,deadline,active_count=80,alpha=2000.0,mu=10.0,max_outer=3,slice_s=35.0):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0) for _ in range(int(n))]
    ac=int(active_count)
    if n>1000: ac=min(ac,150)
    else: ac=min(ac,300)
    for outer in range(int(max_outer)):
       rem=deadline-time.time()
       if rem < 20.0: break
       c=compute_c5_vec(h,n)
       active=select_active(c,n,ac,0.0,cap=ac)
       if len(active)<2: break
       time_sale=min(rem, max(10.0, slice_s))
       dea=time.time()+time_sale
       # maxiter based on time? fixed
       maxiter=300 if ac<=80 else 150
       try:
          res=minimize(active_smooth_obj,h,jac=active_smooth_grad,
                       args=(n,active,float(alpha),float(mu)),
                       method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=bounds,
                       options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-16,'gtol':1e-11,'maxfun':maxiter*2,'maxls':40})
       except Exception:
          break
       h_new=np.asarray(res.x,dtype=float)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(h_new)): break
       h_new=project_feasible(h_new,n)
       if not is_valid_h(h_new,n): break
       c_new=compute_c5(h_new,n)
       if c_new < best_c:
           h=h_new; best_c=c_new; best_h=h.copy()
       # else don't break? maybe break if no improvement and outer small? To allow alpha variation, continue
    return best_h,best_c
```
The `deadline` not used inside minimize except maxiter; okay. If alpha low, maxfun maybe. Good.

`slqp_obj/grad`:
```
def slqp_obj(x,n,active_idx,alpha):
    return active_smooth_obj(x,n,active_idx,alpha,0.0)
def slqp_grad(x,n,active_idx,alpha):
    return active_smooth_grad(x,n,active_idx,alpha,0.0)
def sum_eq(x,n):
    return float(np.sum(x)-n/2.0)
def sum_jac(x,n):
    return np.ones(len(x))
```
`run_slqp_polish`:
```
def run_slqp_polish(h,n,deadline):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
    rem=deadline-time.time()
    if rem < 25.0 or n > 1200:
       return h,best_c
    c=compute_c5_vec(h,n)
    active=select_active(c,n,60 if n>800 else 80,0.0,cap=80)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0) for _ in range(n)]
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':sum_eq,'jac':sum_jac,'args':(n,)}]
    try:
       res=minimize(slqp_obj,h,jac=slqp_grad,args=(n,active,5000.0),
                    method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,
                    options={'maxiter':80,'ftol':1e-12})
       h_new=np.asarray(res.x,dtype=float)
       h_new=project_feasible(h_new,n)
       if is_valid_h(h_new,n):
          c_new=compute_c5(h_new,n)
          if c_new<best_c: return h_new,c_new
    except Exception:
       pass
    return h,best_c
```

`build_jacobian`:
```
def build_jacobian(h,n):
    S=2*int(n)-1
    G=np.zeros((S,n), dtype=float)
    for idx in range(S):
       shift_gradient_into(G[idx], h, n, idx-(n-1))
    return G
```

`pair_line_search` as detailed. Need ensure low/high order. `low = max(-h[i], h[j]-1.0)`; if h[j]-1 negative maybe; `high = min(1-h[i], h[j])`. Include small eps? If high-low<1e-12 return 0.
```
def pair_line_search(c,a,low,high):
    if high <= low + 1e-13: return 0.0
    eps=1e-13
    pos=a>eps; neg=a<-eps
    c_pos=c[pos]; a_pos=a[pos]
    c_neg=c[neg]; a_neg=a[neg]
    zero=~(pos|neg)
    c_zero_max = float(c[zero].max()) if zero.any() else -1.0
    highT=float(c.max())
    lowT=0.0
    # if lowT feasible? maybe not. We binary.
    interval=(low,high)
    # ensure highT feasible numerically: if not, lower? But δ=0 feasible; interval at highT includes 0. Check if not due to numeric, set highT=highT+1e-12 maybe.
    for _ in range(45):
       mid=0.5*(lowT+highT)
       L=low; U=high
       if c_zero_max > mid + 1e-13:
           lowT=mid; continue
       if a_pos.size:
           b=float(np.min((mid-c_pos)/a_pos))
           if b < U: U=b
       if a_neg.size:
           b=float(np.max((mid-c_neg)/a_neg))
           if b > L: L=b
       if L <= U + 1e-14:
           highT=mid; interval=(L,U)
       else:
           lowT=mid
    L,U=interval
    if L <= 0.0 <= U:
       return 0.0
    if L > 0.0:
       return float(L)
    return float(U)
```
Potential problem: If c_zero_max = -1 but zero false. If zero true but c_zero_max > mid. Good.
Could highT not feasible if active c max but interval at δ=0 includes 0? For T=C, for pos: (C-c)/a, since C>=c, a>0 -> nonnegative. U=high≥0, so bound≥0, U remains ≥0. For neg: (C-c)/a, numerator≥0, a<0 -> nonpositive; L=low≤0, so bound ≤0, L remains≤0. zero: c≤C. So feasible. Good.

`run_pair_polish`:
```
def run_pair_polish(h,n,deadline,pair_count=15,rebuild_every=5,max_sweeps=3):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    best_c=compute_c5(h,n); best_h=h.copy()
    rem=deadline-time.time()
    if rem < 80.0 or n > 1500:
        return best_h,best_c
    c=compute_c5_vec(h,n)
    G=build_jacobian(h,n)
    for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
        rem=deadline-time.time()
        if rem < 45.0: break
        active=select_active(c,n,50,0.003,cap=100)
        if len(active)<2: break
        fa=c[active]
        m=float(fa.max())
        sh=5000.0*(fa-m)
        w=np.exp(sh); w/=w.sum()
        g=G[active].T @ w
        order=np.argsort(g)
        low_idx=order[:pair_count]
        high_idx=order[::-1][:pair_count]
        # ensure unique and maybe include random? 
        accepted=0
        # Create pair list high x low
        for i in high_idx:
            for j in low_idx:
                if i==j: continue
                rem=deadline-time.time()
                if rem < 20.0: break
                lo=max(-h[i], h[j]-1.0)
                hi=min(1.0-h[i], h[j])
                if hi <= lo + 1e-12: continue
                a=G[:,i]-G[:,j]
                delta=pair_line_search(c,a,lo,hi)
                if abs(delta) < 1e-10: continue
                h[i]+=delta; h[j]-=delta
                # clip with correction if bounds violation due to rounding
                if h[i] < 0.0:
                    h[j] += h[i]; h[i]=0.0
                elif h[i] > 1.0:
                    h[j] -= h[i]-1.0; h[i]=1.0
                if h[j] < 0.0:
                    h[i] += h[j]; h[j]=0.0
                elif h[j] > 1.0:
                    h[i] -= h[j]-1.0; h[j]=1.0
                if not (0<=h[i]<=1 and 0<=h[j]<=1):
                    h[i]=float(np.clip(h[i],0,1)); h[j]=float(np.clip(h[j],0,1)); # sum may off
                    h=project_feasible(h,n)
                c2=compute_c5(h,n)
                if c2 < best_c - 1e-13:
                    best_c=c2; best_h=h.copy(); c=compute_c5_vec(h,n); accepted+=1
                    if accepted % rebuild_every == 0:
                        G=build_jacobian(h,n)
                else:
                    # revert? We modified h with delta; if not improve, revert by -delta and project? But if clipping happened, exact revert hard. Use saved h copy before? Better save h_i,h_j.
```

Perturbation functions:
```
def smooth_noise(noise, iterations=3, radius=7):
    for _ in range(iterations):
        kernel = np.ones(2*radius+1, dtype=float) / (2*radius+1)
        noise = np.convolve(noise, kernel, mode='same')
    noise -= noise.mean()
    rms=float(np.sqrt(np.mean(noise*noise)))
    if rms > 1e-15: noise /= rms
    else: noise *= 0.0
    return noise
```
No closures.

`random_move_perturbation(h,n,rng,amp=0.05,count=300)`:

def random_move_perturbation(h,n,rng,amp=0.05,count=300):
    h2=h.copy()
    count=int(count)
    for _ in range(count):
        i=int(rng.integers(0,n)); j=int(rng.integers(0,n))
        if i==j: continue
        d=float(rng.normal(0.0,amp))
        # enforce bounds
        if d>0:
            # move mass from i to j: h_i decreases, h_j increases if d>0? Let's define delta add to i, subtract j
            # We'll use delta = d; h_i += delta, h_j -= delta
            if h2[i]+d >1 or h2[j]-d <0:
                d=max(min(d,1-h2[i],h2[j]),0.0)
        else:
            # d negative
            if h2[i]+d <0 or h2[j]-d >1:
                d=min(max(d,-h2[i],? wait h_j - d >1 => d < h_j-1 (negative). Use d between -h_i and h_j-1.
                   d = max(min(d, h2[j]-1.0), -h2[i])
        h2[i]+=d; h2[j]-=d
    return project_feasible(h2,n)
```
Need ensure d clamp correct. Simpler: Try random transfer amount `t` from i to j: `h2[i]-=t; h2[j]+=t` with t∈[-amp,amp]. Feasibility:
- if t>0: t <= h2[i], t <= 1-h2[j]
- if t<0: -t <= h2[j], -t <= 1-h2[i]
Clamp:
```

   t = min(t, h2[i], 1-h2[j])
else:
   t = max(t, -h2[j], h2[i]-1) # since t >= -hj and t >= hi-1? hi-1 negative.
h2[i]-=t; h2[j]+=t
```

def optimize_resolution(h,n,deadline,mode='full'):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    if not is_valid_h(h,n):
       return h, compute_c5(h,n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
    # sym
    hs=0.5*(h + h[::-1])
    hs=project_feasible(hs,n)
    if is_valid_h(hs,n):
       cs=compute_c5(hs,n)
       if cs<best_c:
          best_c=cs; best_h=hs
    h=best_h

    if mode == 'full':
       lp_list = ((80,0.0,0.08,45.0,80), (150,0.003,0.12,60.0,80), (250,0.01,0.25,60.0,80))

    # LBFGS

    elif mode == 'medium':

    else:
       lb_list = ((80,2000.0,20.0,2))

       rem=deadline-time.time()

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    deadline=t0+float(budget_s)-8.0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    try:
       hpar=np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
       npar=len(hpar)
    except Exception:
       npar=500; hpar=np.full(npar,0.5)
    hpar=project_feasible(hpar,npar)
    if not is_valid_h(hpar,npar):
       hpar=np.full(npar,0.5); hpar=project_feasible(hpar,npar)
    best_h=hpar.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(hpar,npar); best_n=npar
    # sym try at parent
    hs=project_feasible(0.5*(hpar+hpar[::-1]),npar)
    if is_valid_h(hs,npar) and compute_c5(hs,npar)<best_c: update
    # optimize at parent if small enough and time
    if npar <= 1000:
       rem=deadline-time.time()
       if rem > 150:
          t1=min(deadline-400, time.time()+80) # medium? maybe do quick full? parent 500. Use medium to save.
          h0,c0=optimize_resolution(hpar,npar,t1,mode='medium')
          if c0<best_c: update
    # choose work n
    if best_n >= 1500:
       n_work=best_n; h_work=best_h
    else:
       n_work=1200
       if best_n != n_work:
          h_work=upsample_h(best_h,n_work)
          h_work=project_feasible(h_work,n_work)
       else:
          h_work=best_h.copy()
    if not is_valid_h(h_work,n_work): h_work=project_feasible(h_work,n_work)
    c_work=compute_c5(h_work,n_work)
    if c_work<best_c:
       best_h=h_work; best_c=c_work; best_n=n_work
    # main optimize at n_work
    rem=deadline-time.time()
    if rem > 250:
       h2,c2=optimize_resolution(h_work,n_work,deadline-120,mode='full')
       if c2<best_c: update
    # if still not target and time, refine

    if rem > 250 and best_n < 2000:

          if c_ref<best_c: update
    # final enforce best
    best_h=enforce_sum_exact(best_h,best_n)
    best_c=compute_c5(best_h,best_n)
    return best_h,float(best_c),int(best_n)

```
def binary_candidate_from(h,n):
    target=int(round(n/2.0))
    b=(h>0.5).astype(float)
    if b.sum()<target:
       # set largest remaining to 1
       order=np.argsort(-h)
       idxs=order[1-np.where(b==1)[0].size():]
       b[idxs]=1
    elif b.sum()>target:
       # set smallest ones to 0
       order=np.argsort(h)
       idxs=order[:b.sum()-target]
       b[idxs]=0
    return b
```
Need if n odd target .5? Use round. But n even. If already exactly. Evaluate. Could be good. Add to perturbations.

Function:
```
def threshold_binary(h,n):
   target=int(round(n/2.0))
   b=(h>0.5).astype(float)
   cur=int(b.sum())
   if cur<target:
       need=target-cur
       order=np.argsort(-h)
       b[order[:max(0,need)]]=1.0
   elif cur>target:
       excess=cur-target
       order=np.argsort(h)
       b[order[:excess]]=0.0
   return b
```
Then project (sum exact). If h all 0.5, target half of first arbitrary. Good.

```python
import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog


def project_feasible(h, n):
    n = int(n)
    target = n / 2.0
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy(), 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(2000):
        diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = float(np.sum(room))
            if tot < 1e-16:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * room
            h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            tot = float(np.sum(h))
            if tot < 1e-16:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * h
            h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h


def enforce_sum_exact(h, n):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    target = n / 2.0
    for _ in range(30):
        s = float(np.sum(h, dtype=np.float64))
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
            room = 1.0 - h[idx]
            add = min(diff, room - 1e-18)
            if add <= 0.0:
                break
            h[idx] += add
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(h))
            room = h[idx]
            add = diff
            if add < -room + 1e-18:
                add = -room
            if add >= 0.0:
                break
            h[idx] += add
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h


def is_valid_h(h, n):
    n = int(n)
    if h.size != n:
        return False
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
        return False
    if float(np.min(h)) < -1e-9 or float(np.max(h)) > 1.0 + 1e-9:
        return False
    if abs(float(np.sum(h)) - n / 2.0) > 1e-6:
        return False
    return True


def upsample_h(h, n):
    old = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, len(old))
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, int(n))
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, old)


def compute_c5_vec(h, n):
    n = int(n)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full') * dx


def compute_c5(h, n):
    return float(np.max(compute_c5_vec(h, n)))


def select_active(c, n, count, margin=0.0, cap=None):
    n = int(n)
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=float)
    if cap is None:
        cap = min(len(c), max(count + 50, 300))
    count = int(min(count, len(c)))
    order = np.argsort(c)[::-1]
    if margin > 0.0:
        maxv = float(c.max())
        inside = np.where(c >= maxv - margin)[0]
        combined = np.unique(np.concatenate((inside, order[:count])))
        if len(combined) > cap:
            combined = combined[np.argsort(-c[combined])][:cap]
        return combined.astype(int)
    return order[:count].astype(int)


def values_at_shifts(h, n, active_idx):
    n = int(n)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    out = np.empty(len(active_idx), dtype=float)
    for r, idx in enumerate(active_idx):
        k = int(idx - (n - 1))
        if k >= 0:
            m = n - k
            a1 = h[k:k + m]
            a2 = h[:m]
            out[r] = dx * (float(np.sum(a1)) - float(np.dot(a1, a2)))
        else:
            a = -k
            m = n - a
            a1 = h[:m]
            a2 = h[a:a + m]
            out[r] = dx * (float(np.sum(a1)) - float(np.dot(a1, a2)))
    return out


def shift_gradient_into(g, h, n, k):
    n = int(n)
    d = 2.0 / float(n)
    g[:] = 0.0

    lo1 = max(0, k)
    hi1 = min(n, k + n)
    if lo1 < hi1:
        g[lo1:hi1] += d * (1.0 - h[lo1 - k:hi1 - k])

    lo2 = max(0, -k)
    hi2 = min(n, n - k)
    if lo2 < hi2:
        g[lo2:hi2] -= d * h[lo2 + k:hi2 + k]


def grad_rows_active(h, n, active_idx):
    n = int(n)
    p = len(active_idx)
    G = np.zeros((p, n), dtype=float)
    for r, idx in enumerate(active_idx):
        shift_gradient_into(G[r], h, n, int(idx - (n - 1)))
    return G


def logsumexp_weights(c, alpha):
    alpha = float(max(alpha, 1.0))
    m = float(np.max(c))
    sh = alpha * (c - m)
    mx = float(np.max(sh))
    expsh = np.exp(sh - mx)
    s = float(np.sum(expsh))
    if s <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros_like(c, dtype=float)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return m, w
    w = expsh / s
    val = m + (mx + float(np.log(s))) / alpha
    return float(val), w


def active_smooth_obj(x, n, active_idx, alpha, mu):
    n = int(n)
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    if len(active_idx) == 0:
        val = 0.0
    else:
        c = values_at_shifts(x, n, active_idx)
        val, _ = logsumexp_weights(c, alpha)
    if mu != 0.0:
        val += float(mu) * float((x.sum() - n / 2.0) ** 2)
    return float(val)


def active_smooth_grad(x, n, active_idx, alpha, mu):
    n = int(n)
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    if len(active_idx) == 0:
        grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    else:
        c = values_at_shifts(x, n, active_idx)
        _, w = logsumexp_weights(c, alpha)
        G = grad_rows_active(x, n, active_idx)
        grad = G.T @ w
    if mu != 0.0:
        grad = grad + 2.0 * float(mu) * float(x.sum() - n / 2.0) * np.ones(n, dtype=float)
    return grad


def local_lp_step(h, n, active_idx, trust=0.1):
    n = int(n)
    if len(active_idx) < 2:
        return None
    G = grad_rows_active(h, n, active_idx)
    F = values_at_shifts(h, n, active_idx)
    m = len(active_idx)

    A_ub = np.empty((m, n + 1), dtype=float)
    A_ub[:, :n] = G
    A_ub[:, n] = -1.0
    b_ub = -F

    c_obj = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=float)
    c_obj[n] = 1.0

    A_eq = np.zeros((1, n + 1), dtype=float)
    A_eq[0, :n] = 1.0
    b_eq = np.zeros(1, dtype=float)

    trust = float(max(trust, 1e-6))
    lb = np.maximum(-h, -trust)
    ub = np.minimum(1.0 - h, trust)
    lb_full = np.empty(n + 1, dtype=float)
    ub_full = np.empty(n + 1, dtype=float)
    lb_full[:n] = lb
    ub_full[:n] = ub
    lb_full[n] = -2.0
    ub_full[n] = 2.0
    bounds = list(zip(lb_full, ub_full))

    res = None
    try:
        res = linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq,
                      bounds=bounds, method='highs')
    except Exception:
        try:
            res = linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq,
                          bounds=bounds)
        except Exception:
            return None

    ok = getattr(res, 'success', False)
    if not ok:
        st = getattr(res, 'status', None)
        ok = st in (0, 2)

    if not ok:
        return None

    delta = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=float)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(delta)):
        return None
    delta = np.clip(delta, -h, 1.0 - h)
    delta -= float(np.mean(delta))
    if float(np.max(np.abs(delta))) < 1e-12:
        return None
    return delta


def line_search_delta(h, n, best_c, delta):
    n = int(n)
    alphas = (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625, 0.03125)
    for a in alphas:
        h2 = h + a * delta
        h2 = project_feasible(h2, n)
        if not is_valid_h(h2, n):
            continue
        c2 = compute_c5(h2, n)
        if c2 < best_c - 1e-13:
            return h2, c2, True
    return h.copy(), best_c, False


def run_lp(h, n, deadline, lp_k=120, margin=0.0, trust=0.1, max_iter=100):
    n = int(n)
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    if not is_valid_h(h, n):
        return h, compute_c5(h, n)

    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(h, n)

    if n > 1500:
        lp_k_eff = min(int(lp_k), 120)
    elif n > 1000:
        lp_k_eff = min(int(lp_k), 200)
    else:
        lp_k_eff = min(int(lp_k), 300)

    for _ in range(int(max_iter)):
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        if rem < 8.0:
            break

        c = compute_c5_vec(h, n)
        cur = float(c.max())
        if cur < best_c:
            best_c = cur
            best_h = h.copy()

        active = select_active(c, n, lp_k_eff, margin, cap=min(len(c), lp_k_eff + 100))
        if len(active) < 2:
            break

        delta = local_lp_step(h, n, active, trust)
        if delta is None:
            if lp_k_eff > 20:
                delta = local_lp_step(h, n, active[:max(2, min(20, len(active)))],
                                      min(0.5, trust * 1.5))
            if delta is None:
                break

        h2, c2, ok = line_search_delta(h, n, best_c, delta)
        if ok:
            h = h2
            best_c = c2
            best_h = h.copy()
            continue

        if trust < 0.4:
            delta2 = local_lp_step(h, n, active, min(0.6, trust * 2.0))
            if delta2 is not None:
                h3, c3, ok2 = line_search_delta(h, n, best_c, delta2)
                if ok2:
                    h = h3
                    best_c = c3
                    best_h = h.copy()
                    continue
        break

    return best_h, best_c


def run_active_lbfgs(h, n, deadline, active_count=80, alpha=2000.0,
                     mu=10.0, max_outer=3, slice_s=35.0):
    n = int(n)
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    if not is_valid_h(h, n):
        return h, compute_c5(h, n)

    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(h, n)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0) for _ in range(n)]

    ac = int(active_count)
    if n > 1500:
        ac = min(ac, 120)
    elif n > 1000:
        ac = min(ac, 150)
    else:
        ac = min(ac, 300)

    for _ in range(int(max_outer)):
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        if rem < 20.0:
            break
        c = compute_c5_vec(h, n)
        active = select_active(c, n, ac, 0.0, cap=ac)
        if len(active) < 2:
            break

        if ac <= 80:
            maxiter = 250
        else:
            maxiter = 130

        try:
            res = minimize(active_smooth_obj, h, jac=active_smooth_grad,
                           args=(n, active, float(alpha), float(mu)),
                           method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds,
                           options={'maxiter': maxiter,
                                    'ftol': 1e-16,
                                    'gtol': 1e-11,
                                    'maxfun': maxiter * 2,
                                    'maxls': 40})
        except Exception:
            break

        h_new = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(h_new)):
            break
        h_new = project_feasible(h_new, n)
        if not is_valid_h(h_new, n):
            break
        c_new = compute_c5(h_new, n)
        if c_new < best_c:
            h = h_new
            best_c = c_new
            best_h = h.copy()

    return best_h, best_c


def slqp_obj(x, n, active_idx, alpha):
    return active_smooth_obj(x, n, active_idx, alpha, 0.0)


def slqp_grad(x, n, active_idx, alpha):
    return active_smooth_grad(x, n, active_idx, alpha, 0.0)


def sum_eq(x, n):
    return float(np.sum(x) - n / 2.0)


def sum_jac(x, n):
    return np.ones(len(x), dtype=float)


def run_slqp_polish(h, n, deadline):
    n = int(n)
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_c = compute_c5(h, n)
    rem = deadline - time.time()
    if rem < 25.0 or n > 900:
        return h, best_c

    c = compute_c5_vec(h, n)
    active = select_active(c, n, 60, 0.0, cap=60)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0) for _ in range(n)]
    cons = [{'type': 'eq', 'fun': sum_eq, 'jac': sum_jac, 'args': (n,)}]

    try:
        res = minimize(slqp_obj, h, jac=slqp_grad,
                       args=(n, active, 5000.0),
                       method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons,
                       options={'maxiter': 70, 'ftol': 1e-12})
        h_new = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
        h_new = project_feasible(h_new, n)
        if is_valid_h(h_new, n):
            c_new = compute_c5(h_new, n)
            if c_new < best_c:
                return h_new, c_new
    except Exception:
        pass
    return h, best_c


def build_jacobian(h, n):
    n = int(n)
    S = 2 * n - 1
    G = np.zeros((S, n), dtype=float)
    for idx in range(S):
        shift_gradient_into(G[idx], h, n, idx - (n - 1))
    return G


def pair_line_search(c, a, low, high):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=float)
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    if high <= low + 1e-13:
        return 0.0

    eps = 1e-13
    pos = a > eps
    neg = a < -eps
    zero = ~(pos | neg)

    c_pos = c[pos]
    a_pos = a[pos]
    c_neg = c[neg]
    a_neg = a[neg]
    c_zero_max = float(c[zero].max()) if zero.any() else -1.0

    lowT = 0.0
    highT = float(c.max())
    interval = (low, high)

    for _ in range(45):
        mid = 0.5 * (lowT + highT)
        L = low
        U = high

        if c_zero_max > mid + 1e-13:
            lowT = mid
            continue

        if a_pos.size:
            b = float(np.min((mid - c_pos) / a_pos))
            if b < U:
                U = b
        if a_neg.size:
            b = float(np.max((mid - c_neg) / a_neg))
            if b > L:
                L = b

        if L <= U + 1e-14:
            highT = mid
            interval = (L, U)
        else:
            lowT = mid

    L, U = interval
    if L <= 0.0 <= U:
        return 0.0
    if L > 0.0:
        return float(L)
    return float(U)


def run_pair_polish(h, n, deadline, pair_count=15, rebuild_every=5, max_sweeps=3):
    n = int(n)
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(h, n)

    rem = deadline - time.time()
    if rem < 80.0 or n > 1500:
        return best_h, best_c

    c = compute_c5_vec(h, n)
    G = build_jacobian(h, n)

    for _ in range(int(max_sweeps)):
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        if rem < 45.0:
            break

        active = select_active(c, n, 50, 0.003, cap=100)
        if len(active) < 2:
            break

        fa = c[active]
        m = float(fa.max())
        w = np.exp(5000.0 * (fa - m))
        w /= np.sum(w)
        g = G[active].T @ w

        order = np.argsort(g)
        low_idx = order[:pair_count]
        high_idx = order[::-1][:pair_count]
        accepted = 0

        for i in high_idx:
            for j in low_idx:
                if i == j:
                    continue
                rem = deadline - time.time()
                if rem < 20.0:
                    break

                lo = max(-h[i], h[j] - 1.0)
                hi = min(1.0 - h[i], h[j])
                if hi <= lo + 1e-12:
                    continue

                a = G[:, i] - G[:, j]
                delta = pair_line_search(c, a, lo, hi)
                if abs(delta) < 1e-10:
                    continue

                old_i = h[i]
                old_j = h[j]
                new_i = old_i + delta
                new_j = old_j - delta

                if (new_i < -1e-11 or new_i > 1.0 + 1e-11 or
                        new_j < -1e-11 or new_j > 1.0 + 1e-11):
                    continue

                h[i] = float(np.clip(new_i, 0.0, 1.0))
                h[j] = float(np.clip(new_j, 0.0, 1.0))

                c2 = compute_c5(h, n)
                if c2 < best_c - 1e-13:
                    best_c = c2
                    best_h = h.copy()
                    c = compute_c5_vec(h, n)
                    accepted += 1
                    if accepted % int(rebuild_every) == 0:
                        G = build_jacobian(h, n)
                else:
                    h[i] = old_i
                    h[j] = old_j

        if accepted == 0:
            break
        G = build_jacobian(h, n)
        c = compute_c5_vec(h, n)

    return best_h, best_c


def smooth_noise(noise, iterations=3, radius=7):
    noise = np.asarray(noise, dtype=float).copy()
    radius = int(max(1, radius))
    kernel = np.ones(2 * radius + 1, dtype=float) / float(2 * radius + 1)
    for _ in range(int(iterations)):
        noise = np.convolve(noise, kernel, mode='same')
    noise -= float(np.mean(noise))
    rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(noise * noise)))
    if rms > 1e-15:
        noise /= rms
    else:
        noise[:] = 0.0
    return noise


def random_move_perturbation(h, n, rng, amp=0.05, count=300):
    n = int(n)
    h2 = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    count = int(count)
    amp = float(amp)
    for _ in range(count):
        i = int(rng.integers(0, n))
        j = int(rng.integers(0, n))
        if i == j:
            continue
        t = float(rng.normal(0.0, amp))
        if t > 0.0:
            t = min(t, h2[i], 1.0 - h2[j])
        else:
            t = max(t, h2[i] - 1.0, -h2[j])
        h2[i] -= t
        h2[j] += t
    return project_feasible(h2, n)


def threshold_binary(h, n):
    n = int(n)
    target = int(round(n / 2.0))
    b = (h > 0.5).astype(float)
    cur = int(b.sum())
    if cur < target:
        need = target - cur
        if need > 0:
            order = np.argsort(-h)
            b[order[:need]] = 1.0
    elif cur > target:
        excess = cur - target
        if excess > 0:
            order = np.argsort(h)
            b[order[:excess]] = 0.0
    return b


def optimize_resolution(h, n, deadline, mode='full'):
    n = int(n)
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    if not is_valid_h(h, n):
        return h, compute_c5(h, n)

    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(h, n)

    hs = 0.5 * (h + h[::-1])
    hs = project_feasible(hs, n)
    if is_valid_h(hs, n):
        cs = compute_c5(hs, n)
        if cs < best_c:
            best_c = cs
            best_h = hs.copy()
    h = best_h

    if mode == 'full':
        lp_list = (
            (80, 0.0, 0.08, 45.0, 80),
            (150, 0.003, 0.12, 60.0, 80),
            (250, 0.01, 0.25, 60.0, 80),
        )
    elif mode == 'medium':
        lp_list = (
            (80, 0.0, 0.08, 30.0, 50),
            (150, 0.005, 0.2, 40.0, 50),
        )
    else:
        lp_list = (
            (80, 0.0, 0.08, 18.0, 30),
        )

    for count, margin, trust, slice_s, max_iter in lp_list:
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        if rem < slice_s + 10.0:
            break
        dea = min(deadline, time.time() + slice_s)
        h2, c2 = run_lp(h, n, dea, lp_k=count, margin=margin, trust=trust, max_iter=max_iter)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_c = c2
            best_h = h2
        h = best_h

    if mode == 'full':
        lb_list = (
            (40, 500.0, 3.0, 30.0),
            (80, 2000.0, 3.0, 40.0),
            (150, 8000.0, 2.0, 45.0),
            (300, 20000.0, 1.0, 45.0),
        )
    elif mode == 'medium':
        lb_list = (
            (60, 1000.0, 2.0, 25.0),
            (150, 5000.0, 2.0, 35.0),
        )
    else:
        lb_list = (
            (80, 2000.0, 2.0, 20.0),
        )

    for count, alpha, max_outer, slice_s in lb_list:
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        if rem < 30.0:
            break
        h2, c2 = run_active_lbfgs(
            h, n, deadline,
            active_count=count, alpha=alpha, mu=10.0,
            max_outer=max_outer, slice_s=slice_s
        )
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_c = c2
            best_h = h2
        h = best_h

    if mode == 'full':
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        if rem > 70.0:
            h2, c2 = run_lp(h, n, deadline - 25.0, lp_k=180, margin=0.005, trust=0.1, max_iter=60)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_c = c2
                best_h = h2
            h = best_h

    if mode == 'full':
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        if rem > 150.0:
            h2, c2 = run_pair_polish(h, n, deadline - 50.0,
                                     pair_count=15, rebuild_every=5, max_sweeps=3)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_c = c2
                best_h = h2
            h = best_h

    if mode in ('full', 'medium'):
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        if rem > 80.0 and n <= 900:
            h2, c2 = run_slqp_polish(h, n, deadline - 5.0)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_c = c2
                best_h = h2

    best_h = enforce_sum_exact(best_h, n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n)
    return best_h, best_c


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + float(budget_s) - 8.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    try:
        hpar = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        npar = len(hpar)
    except Exception:
        npar = 500
        hpar = np.full(npar, 0.5, dtype=float)

    hpar = project_feasible(hpar, npar)
    if not is_valid_h(hpar, npar):
        hpar = np.full(npar, 0.5, dtype=float)
        hpar = project_feasible(hpar, npar)

    best_h = hpar.copy()
    best_c = float(compute_c5(hpar, npar))
    best_n = int(npar)

    hs = project_feasible(0.5 * (best_h + best_h[::-1]), best_n)
    if is_valid_h(hs, best_n):
        cs = compute_c5(hs, best_n)
        if cs < best_c:
            best_c = cs
            best_h = hs.copy()

    if best_n <= 1000 and deadline - time.time() > 140.0:
        parent_deadline = min(time.time() + 90.0, deadline - 500.0)
        h0, c0 = optimize_resolution(best_h, best_n, parent_deadline, mode='medium')
        if c0 < best_c:
            best_c = c0
            best_h = h0.copy()
            best_n = int(len(best_h))

    if best_n >= 1500:
        n_work = best_n
        h_work = best_h.copy()
    else:
        n_work = 1200
        if best_n == n_work:
            h_work = best_h.copy()
        else:
            h_work = upsample_h(best_h, n_work)
        h_work = project_feasible(h_work, n_work)

    if is_valid_h(h_work, n_work):
        cw = float(compute_c5(h_work, n_work))
        if cw < best_c:
            best_c = cw
            best_h = h_work.copy()
            best_n = n_work

    main_deadline = deadline - 300.0
    if deadline - time.time() > 250.0:
        h2, c2 = optimize_resolution(h_work, n_work, main_deadline, mode='full')
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_c = c2
            best_h = h2.copy()
            best_n = int(len(best_h))

    rem = deadline - time.time()
    if rem > 220.0 and best_n < 2000:
        n_ref = 2000
        h_r = upsample_h(best_h, n_ref)
        h_r = project_feasible(h_r, n_ref)
        if is_valid_h(h_r, n_ref):
            cr = compute_c5(h_r, n_ref)
            if cr < best_c + 0.005:
                mode = 'medium' if rem > 330.0 else 'light'
                h_r, c_r = optimize_resolution(h_r, n_ref, deadline - 40.0, mode=mode)
                if c_r < best_c:
                    best_c = c_r
                    best_h = h_r.copy()
                    best_n = int(len(best_h))

    rem = deadline - time.time()
    if rem > 130.0:
        n_rest = n_work
        if best_n == n_rest:
            h_rest = best_h.copy()
        else:
            h_rest = upsample_h(best_h, n_rest)
        h_rest = project_feasible(h_rest, n_rest)

        for r in range(3):
            rem = deadline - time.time()
            if rem < 85.0:
                break

            if r == 0:
                hc = threshold_binary(h_rest, n_rest)
            elif r % 2 == 0:
                amp = 0.05 + 0.03 * float(r)
                noise = smooth_noise(rng.normal(size=(n_rest,)), 3, 7)
                hc = h_rest + amp * noise
            else:
                amp = 0.04 + 0.02 * float(r)
                hc = random_move_perturbation(h_rest, n_rest, rng, amp=amp, count=400)

            hc = project_feasible(hc, n_rest)
            if not is_valid_h(hc, n_rest):
                continue

            hs = project_feasible(0.5 * (hc + hc[::-1]), n_rest)
            chc = hc
            chc_c = compute_c5(hc, n_rest)
            if is_valid_h(hs, n_rest):
                hsc_c = compute_c5(hs, n_rest)
                if hsc_c < chc_c:
                    chc = hs
                    chc_c = hsc_c

            if chc_c > best_c + 0.004:
                continue

            rem = deadline - time.time()
            if rem < 55.0:
                break

            hc = project_feasible(chc, n_rest)
            hc2, cc2 = run_lp(hc, n_rest, time.time() + 22.0,
                              lp_k=100, margin=0.004, trust=0.05, max_iter=40)

            rem = deadline - time.time()
            if rem < 40.0:
                if cc2 < best_c:
                    best_c = cc2
                    best_h = hc2.copy()
                    best_n = int(len(best_h))
                continue

            hc2, cc2 = run_active_lbfgs(
                hc2, n_rest, time.time() + 35.0,
                active_count=80, alpha=3000.0, mu=10.0,
                max_outer=2, slice_s=15.0
            )
            if cc2 < best_c:
                best_c = cc2
                best_h = hc2.copy()
                best_n = int(len(best_h))

    best_h = enforce_sum_exact(best_h, best_n)
    best_c = float(compute_c5(best_h, best_n))
    return best_h, best_c, int(len(best_h))
```
